| strict 块互质动力容量反超路由 | 新增/直接矛盾判据闭合，rough 负载反超仍开放 | 新增 `experiments/prime_matrix_strict_block_coprime_dynamics_capacity_surplus_router.py` 与 `docs/monograph/prime-matrix-strict-block-coprime-dynamics-capacity-surplus-router.md/json`。审查确认 `exact_block_capacity_formula_closed=true`、`full_row_alpha_half_capacity_closed=true`、`direct_capacity_surplus_contradiction_criterion_closed=true`、`adjacent_quotient_dynamics_integrated=true`、`coprime_dynamics_capacity_surplus_proved=false`、`finite_supply_vs_load_contradiction_proved=false`、`direct_unconditional_contradiction_found=false`、`row_column_unconditional_closed=false`：对任意块 `B` 与 cutoff `z`，早期零行必要条件为 `|R_{B,z}(x)|<=C_{B,z}(x)`，其中 `C_{B,z}` 是 `(z,P)` 高标签在块内的精确命中容量；若反向严格不等式成立则立即矛盾。特别地 `z=alpha P, alpha>1/2` 时全行容量 `<=2(pi(P)-pi(alpha P))`。相邻互质和商相邻互质已接入壳层容量，但它们本身不提供 rough 负载下界；最新最窄输入为 `AlphaPrefixRoughLoadLowerBoundExceedingHighLabelCapacity`，并行保留 `B3RemainderTotalVariationBudgetForLengthP` 与 quotient-shell 缺陷账本。 |
| strict alpha-prefix 负载缺陷/PDEC 路由 | 新增/容量失败强制 TV 或端点缺陷 | 新增 `experiments/prime_matrix_strict_alpha_prefix_load_deficit_pdec_router.py` 与 `docs/monograph/prime-matrix-strict-alpha-prefix-load-deficit-pdec-router.md/json`。审查确认 `capacity_failure_to_tv_defect_implication_closed=true`、`pdec_registration_route_defined=true`、`alpha_prefix_load_lower_bound_proved=false`、`alpha_prefix_tv_defect_excluded=false`、`direct_unconditional_contradiction_found=false`、`row_column_unconditional_closed=false`：对 `alpha>1/2`，早期零行给出 `|R_alpha(x)|<=2(pi(P)-pi(alpha P))`，而 prefix lower-weight 公式给出 `|R_alpha(x)|>=(P-1)W^-_alpha-TV_alpha`；因此若容量反超没有直接发生，反例必须满足 `TV_alpha >= (P-1)W^-_alpha-2(pi(P)-pi(alpha P))`，即强端点/PDEC/SAE 缺陷。最新最窄输入为 `AlphaPrefixTotalVariationOrEndpointPDECDefectExclusion`；并行仍需 `B3ContinuousBetaSieveCoefficientSurplusAlpha043AndDiscretePrimeSumUniformError`、`B3RemainderTotalVariationBudgetForLengthP`、`FiniteBoundaryPrefixRoughCountCertificate`。 |
| strict alpha-prefix 有符号端点缺陷分裂 | 新增/TV缺陷加强为 signed endpoint PDEC 三出口 | 新增 `experiments/prime_matrix_strict_alpha_prefix_signed_endpoint_defect_split_router.py` 与 `docs/monograph/prime-matrix-strict-alpha-prefix-signed-endpoint-defect-split-router.md/json`。审查确认 `signed_endpoint_defect_forced=true`、`low_middle_far_split_closed=true`、`dyadic_pigeonhole_closed=true`、`high_dyadic_endpoint_lock_imported=true`、`signed_endpoint_defect_excluded=false`、`direct_unconditional_contradiction_found=false`、`row_column_unconditional_closed=false`：lower-weight 和可精确写为 `L_alpha=(P-1)W^-_alpha+E_alpha`；若早期零行使 `R_alpha<=C_alpha` 且主项间隙 `G_alpha=(P-1)W^-_alpha-C_alpha>0`，则强制有符号端点缺陷 `E_alpha<=-G_alpha`。再按低模、中间 dyadic、远尾 core 分裂；若低模和远尾不能承担固定比例负缺陷，则某个 dyadic 块给出 PDEC 证书。最新最窄输入为 `DyadicLowerWeightEndpointPDECExclusion`，并行保留 `LowModSignedEndpointDefectExclusion`、`LowerWeightFarTailCoreOrSAEExclusion`、`AlphaLowerWeightMainGapPositiveAgainstHighLabelCapacity`。 |
| strict 加权 dyadic endpoint PDEC/HDL 接桥 | 新增/lower-weight 口径接入 HDL/CoreLoad/cofactor | 新增 `experiments/prime_matrix_strict_weighted_dyadic_endpoint_pdec_hdl_router.py` 与 `docs/monograph/prime-matrix-strict-weighted-dyadic-endpoint-pdec-hdl-router.md/json`。审查确认 `weighted_hdl_bridge_closed=true`、`high_block_single_hit_coreload_closed=true`、`weighted_cofactor_band_projection_closed=true`、`dyadic_endpoint_pdec_excluded=false`、`direct_unconditional_contradiction_found=false`、`row_column_unconditional_closed=false`：当前 alpha-prefix dyadic 缺陷带有 lower-weight，不能直接套用 Möbius 裸权重 HDL；本步将其拆成正权端点命中亏损或负权端点命中过剩。高块 `d>P-1` 时端点命中为单命中 CRT 事件，并等价于逐列低素核加权 coreload；过剩分支还可反演到 `m<P+2` 的互补因子带。最新最窄输入为 `WeightedPositiveEndpointHitDeficitPDEC`，并行保留 `WeightedNegativeEndpointHitSurplusCoreLoad`、`WeightedCofactorLoadCapacityOrColumnCRT`、`LowOrMiddleDyadicEndpointPDECExclusion`、`LowerWeightFarTailCoreOrSAEExclusion`、`AlphaLowerWeightMainGapPositiveAgainstHighLabelCapacity`。 |
| strict 正权端点亏损 Fourier/PDEC 输入化 | 新增/正权亏损转成中心化零均值 Fourier 证书 | 新增 `experiments/prime_matrix_strict_weighted_positive_endpoint_deficit_fourier_pdec_router.py` 与 `docs/monograph/prime-matrix-strict-weighted-positive-endpoint-deficit-fourier-pdec-router.md/json`。审查确认 `centered_fourier_pdec_object_closed=true`、`fourier_energy_lower_bound_closed=true`、`persistent_or_sae_route_defined=true`、`weighted_positive_endpoint_deficit_excluded=false`、`direct_unconditional_contradiction_found=false`、`row_column_unconditional_closed=false`：正权端点亏损可写为完整 CRT 周期上的零均值函数 `Phi_I^+`；若 `Phi_I^+(x0)<=-kappa`，则非零 Fourier 能量至少为 `kappa^2/(Q_I-1)`。持久亏损进入 H4/PDEC，孤立亏损进入 SAE。最新最窄输入为 `WeightedPositiveEndpointFourierPDECUpperBound`，并行保留 `DeficitPersistenceOrSAEClassification`、`PositiveWeightSupportLowComplexityLedger`、`WeightedNegativeEndpointHitSurplusCoreLoad`。 |
| strict 正权端点 Fourier 上界分裂 | 新增/上界压成低有效模、几何衰减、Bohr-cap 三出口 | 新增 `experiments/prime_matrix_strict_weighted_positive_endpoint_fourier_upper_router.py` 与 `docs/monograph/prime-matrix-strict-weighted-positive-endpoint-fourier-upper-router.md/json`。审查确认 `fourier_coefficient_formula_closed=true`、`low_effective_mod_route_closed=true`、`large_effective_mod_decay_template_closed=true`、`bohrcap_large_spectrum_route_registered=true`、`weighted_positive_endpoint_fourier_upper_bound_proved=false`、`direct_unconditional_contradiction_found=false`、`row_column_unconditional_closed=false`：单模端点弧 Fourier 系数由几何级数控制；若全局频率在许多 `d` 上低有效分母共振，则这是 `LowEffectiveModEndpointPDECOrColumnCRT`；若无共振，则需用有效分母衰减与正权支撑预算给出上界；预算失败回流 `PositiveWeightSupportLargeSpectrumBohrCapExclusion`。最新最窄输入为 `LowEffectiveModEndpointPDECOrColumnCRT`，并行保留 `EndpointArcGeometricDecayLargeEffectiveModBudget`、`PositiveWeightSupportLargeSpectrumBohrCapExclusion`、`DeficitPersistenceOrSAEClassification`。 |
| strict 低有效模 endpoint PDEC/ColumnCRT 路由 | 新增/低有效模压成共同因子倒数和 | 新增 `experiments/prime_matrix_strict_low_effective_mod_endpoint_pdec_columncrt_router.py` 与 `docs/monograph/prime-matrix-strict-low-effective-mod-endpoint-pdec-columncrt-router.md/json`。审查确认 `effective_gcd_identity_closed=true`、`zero_mode_cancellation_closed=true`、`reciprocal_common_divisor_reduction_closed=true`、`pdec_columncrt_route_registered=true`、`low_effective_mod_excluded=false`、`direct_unconditional_contradiction_found=false`、`row_column_unconditional_closed=false`：因 `gcd(P,d)=1`，有 `gcd(hP^{-1} mod d,d)=gcd(h,d)`；若有效分母 `s=d/gcd(h,d)` 小，则 `d=s g` 且 `g|h`。`s=1` 零频被中心化端点函数删除，`s>=2` 项有 `1/g` 共同因子衰减。最新最窄输入为 `WeightedReciprocalCommonDivisorEnvelopeForLowEffectiveSpectrum`，并行保留 `HotFrequencyCommonDivisorSupportBudget`、`LowQuotientColumnCRTOrPDECRoute`、`EndpointArcGeometricDecayLargeEffectiveModBudget`。 |
| strict 加权共同因子倒数封套 | 新增/共同因子倒数和压成短窗口除数封套 | 新增 `experiments/prime_matrix_strict_weighted_reciprocal_common_divisor_envelope_router.py` 与 `docs/monograph/prime-matrix-strict-weighted-reciprocal-common-divisor-envelope-router.md/json`。审查确认 `pointwise_weight_bound_imported=true`、`windowed_reciprocal_envelope_closed=true`、`hot_window_pigeonhole_closed=true`、`reciprocal_envelope_beats_pdec_lower_bound=false`、`hot_window_excluded=false`、`direct_unconditional_contradiction_found=false`、`row_column_unconditional_closed=false`：positive lower-weight 点态 `<=1`，所以低有效贡献 `<=1/2 sum_{2<=s<=R} sum_{g|h,B/s<g<=2B/s}1/g`。若封套足够小则排除低有效模；若不小，某个小商 `s` 强制出现热频率因子窗口，持久时进入 PDEC/ColumnCRT，孤立时进入 SAE。最新最窄输入为 `WindowedReciprocalDivisorEnvelopeForFrequencyH`。 |
| strict 短窗口倒数除数密度 | 新增/窗口倒数封套压成热除数密度证书 | 新增 `experiments/prime_matrix_strict_windowed_reciprocal_divisor_density_router.py` 与 `docs/monograph/prime-matrix-strict-windowed-reciprocal-divisor-density-router.md/json`。审查确认 `reciprocal_to_divisor_count_closed=true`、`hot_density_certificate_closed=true`、`generic_tau_closure_rejected=true`、`windowed_divisor_envelope_proved=false`、`hot_density_excluded=false`、`direct_unconditional_contradiction_found=false`、`row_column_unconditional_closed=false`：对 `Y<g<=2Y`，`sum_{g|h}1/g<=N_h(Y,2Y]/Y`；若倒数和超过 `eta`，则 `N_h(Y,2Y]>=eta Y`，即热频率除数密度证书。不能用无参数 `tau(h)` 粗估计偷关，必须与 PDEC 下界比较或排斥热密度的 PDEC/ColumnCRT/SAE 出口。最新最窄输入为 `ShortWindowDivisorDensityEnvelopeForFrequencyH`，并行保留 `WeightedPositiveEndpointPDECLowerBoundComparison`、`HotFrequencyDivisorDensityPDECorSAE`、`LowQuotientColumnCRTOrPDECRoute`。 |
| strict 短窗口除数密度 LCM 乘子纪律 | 新增/热除数密度压成 LCM 爆炸或共同核复现 | 新增 `experiments/prime_matrix_strict_short_window_divisor_density_lcm_router.py` 与 `docs/monograph/prime-matrix-strict-short-window-divisor-density-lcm-router.md/json`。审查确认 `short_window_density_materialized=true`、`short_window_antichain_closed=true`、`lcm_anchor_closed=true`、`incremental_multiplier_discipline_closed=true`、`high_multiplier_count_bound_closed=true`、`low_multiplier_kernel_forcing_closed=true`、`low_multiplier_kernel_route_registered=true`、`dense_lcm_after_kernel_compression_proved=false`、`formal_frequency_height_ceiling_matched=false`、`low_multiplier_common_kernel_excluded=false`、`short_window_divisor_density_envelope_proved=false`、`hot_density_excluded=false`、`direct_unconditional_contradiction_found=false`、`row_column_unconditional_closed=false`：若 `A={g:Y<g<=2Y,g|h}` 且 `|A|>=eta Y`，则 `L(A)|h`；按任意顺序加入 `g_t`，新增乘子 `mu_t=L_t/L_{t-1}=g_t/gcd(g_t,L_{t-1})`，若 `L(A)<=H` 则 `#{t:mu_t>=Lambda}<=floor(log H/log Lambda)`，其余大量元素强制 `gcd(g_t,L_{t-1})>Y/Lambda`。因此当前最窄点推进为 `LowMultiplierCommonKernelColumnCRTOrPDECRoute`；备用硬点为 `DenseShortWindowLCMLowerBoundAfterKernelCompression`，并行保留正式频率高度上界和 PDEC 下界比较。 |
| strict 低乘子共同核分流 | 新增/共同核复现压成成对差值锁或多源扇入 | 新增 `experiments/prime_matrix_strict_low_multiplier_common_kernel_router.py` 与 `docs/monograph/prime-matrix-strict-low-multiplier-common-kernel-router.md/json`。审查确认 `low_multiplier_kernel_imported=true`、`kernel_subcover_closed=true`、`pair_or_fanin_dichotomy_closed=true`、`large_pair_difference_lock_closed=true`、`large_pair_columncrt_route_registered=true`、`fanin_sae_pdec_route_registered=true`、`large_pair_columncrt_excluded=false`、`fanin_sae_or_pdec_excluded=false`、`low_multiplier_common_kernel_excluded=false`、`direct_unconditional_contradiction_found=false`、`row_column_unconditional_closed=false`：若低乘子事件给出 `K_t=gcd(g_t,L_{t-1})>Y/Lambda`，则 `K_t|lcm_{i<t}gcd(g_t,g_i)`，大共同核必须由成对 gcd 云覆盖。于是出口被拆成两类：存在大成对共同核 `k` 时，因 `Y<g_i,g_t<=2Y` 得 `k|(g_t-g_i)` 且 `0<|g_t-g_i|<Y`，形成低商差值锁并进入 `ColumnCRT/PDEC`；若无单一大核，则只能由多个旧除数分摊覆盖，形成 `MultiSourceKernelFanInSAEOrPDECExclusion`。当前最窄点为 `LargePairKernelDifferenceColumnCRTExclusion`，并列保留多源扇入 SAE/PDEC 排斥。 |
| strict 大成对共同核差值锁 | 新增/差值锁压成有限商型 PDEC 或有限字母表 SAE | 新增 `experiments/prime_matrix_strict_large_pair_kernel_difference_router.py` 与 `docs/monograph/prime-matrix-strict-large-pair-kernel-difference-router.md/json`。审查确认 `exact_pair_gcd_normal_form_closed=true`、`bounded_quotient_gap_closed=true`、`finite_quotient_alphabet_closed=true`、`fixed_type_pdec_route_registered=true`、`bounded_type_sae_route_registered=true`、`fixed_type_pdec_excluded=false`、`bounded_type_sae_absorbed=false`、`large_pair_kernel_difference_excluded=false`、`direct_unconditional_contradiction_found=false`、`row_column_unconditional_closed=false`：对成对短窗口除数取精确共同核 `k=gcd(g,g')`，写 `g=kb,g'=k(b+a)`，则 `gcd(b,b+a)=1`；若 `k>Y/Lambda`，由 `Y<g,g'<=2Y` 得 `1<=b,b+a<2Lambda` 且 `0<|a|<Lambda`，所以所有大核差值锁只落在 `O(Lambda^2)` 个有限商型 `(b,a)` 中。持久同型复现进入 `FixedQuotientTypeColumnCRTOrPDECExclusion`；非持久同型进入 `BoundedQuotientTypeSAEAbsorption`。 |
| strict 固定商型 ColumnCRT/PDEC 路由 | 新增/固定商型压成缩频核心除数递归或 PDEC | 新增 `experiments/prime_matrix_strict_fixed_quotient_type_columncrt_router.py` 与 `docs/monograph/prime-matrix-strict-fixed-quotient-type-columncrt-router.md/json`。审查确认 `fixed_type_scaled_frequency_identity_closed=true`、`core_interval_bounds_closed=true`、`strict_height_descent_closed=true`、`finite_descent_depth_closed=true`、`single_unit_density_to_scaled_core_closed=true`、`fixed_type_pdec_route_registered=true`、`density_transfer_without_loss_proved=false`、`fixed_type_pdec_excluded=false`、`fixed_quotient_type_excluded=false`、`direct_unconditional_contradiction_found=false`、`row_column_unconditional_closed=false`：固定商型 `(b,a)` 记 `c=b+a`，由 `g=kb,g'=kc,gcd(b,c)=1` 得 `g|h` 且 `g'|h` 当且仅当 `kbc|h`，即 `k|h/(bc)`；原窗口给出 `Y/max(b,c)<k<=2Y/min(b,c)`。又合法商型有 `bc>=2`，所以同型递归每步使频率高度至少折半，链长 `<=floor(log_2|h|)`，切断无限循环。当前最窄点更新为 `FixedQuotientDensityTransferWithoutLoss`，并列保留 `FixedQuotientTypePDECColumnCertificateExclusion`。 |
| strict 固定商型密度传递 | 新增/pair 到 core 的显式损耗账本闭合 | 新增 `experiments/prime_matrix_strict_fixed_quotient_density_transfer_router.py` 与 `docs/monograph/prime-matrix-strict-fixed-quotient-density-transfer-router.md/json`。审查确认 `core_pair_bijection_closed=true`、`finite_type_pigeonhole_loss_closed=true`、`core_window_distortion_bound_closed=true`、`density_transfer_without_loss_closed=true`、`no_untracked_weight_loss_closed=true`、`iterated_threshold_survival_proved=false`、`fixed_pdec_excluded=false`、`fixed_quotient_type_excluded=false`、`direct_unconditional_contradiction_found=false`、`row_column_unconditional_closed=false`：固定互素商型 `(b,c)` 下，`k -> (kb,kc)` 一一对应，商型内部无计数损失；未固定商型时，有限字母表 `A_Lambda<=8Lambda^2` 给出显式鸽巢损失，总 pair-lock 密度至少有一个商型保留 `1/A_Lambda`。有向/无向口径常数并入该账本。因此 `FixedQuotientDensityTransferWithoutLoss` 已闭合，最新最窄点为 `IteratedScaledCoreDensitySurvivalOrPDECEscape`；并列仍需 `FixedQuotientTypePDECColumnCertificateExclusion`。 |
| strict 迭代缩频核心密度账本 | 新增/缩频链三出口闭合，剩阈值坍缩排斥 | 新增 `experiments/prime_matrix_strict_iterated_scaled_core_density_router.py` 与 `docs/monograph/prime-matrix-strict-iterated-scaled-core-density-router.md/json`。审查确认 `scaled_frequency_product_law_closed=true`、`strict_height_decay_closed=true`、`density_loss_product_ledger_closed=true`、`window_scale_product_law_closed=true`、`three_exit_ledger_closed=true`、`iterated_threshold_collapse_excluded=false`、`depthwise_lcm_explosion_proved=false`、`fixed_type_pdec_excluded=false`、`iterated_scaled_core_density_survival_proved=false`、`direct_unconditional_contradiction_found=false`、`row_column_unconditional_closed=false`：固定商型链满足 `h_r=h_0/prod_{i<=r}b_i c_i` 且 `|h_r|<=|h_0|/2^r`；密度损耗满足 `rho_r>=rho_0/prod A_{Lambda_i}`、`A_{Lambda_i}<=8Lambda_i^2`，窗口尺度也有显式乘积账本。递归因此只有三出口：某层 LCM 高度矛盾、固定商型 ColumnCRT/PDEC 逃逸、或阈值过早坍缩。最新最窄点为 `IteratedThresholdCollapseExclusionLedger`，并列保留 `FixedQuotientTypePDECColumnCertificateExclusion` 与 `DepthwiseLCMExplosionAgainstScaledFrequencyHeight`。 |
| strict 迭代阈值坍缩账本 | 新增/坍缩压成稀疏终端历史 PDEC 或 SAE | 新增 `experiments/prime_matrix_strict_iterated_threshold_collapse_router.py` 与 `docs/monograph/prime-matrix-strict-iterated-threshold-collapse-router.md/json`。审查确认 `effective_core_lower_bound_closed=true`、`collapse_inequality_closed=true`、`adaptive_lambda_balance_registered=true`、`sparse_terminal_reduction_closed=true`、`persistent_history_pdec_route_registered=true`、`nonpersistent_history_sae_route_registered=true`、`threshold_collapse_excluded=false`、`sparse_terminal_history_absorbed=false`、`fixed_type_history_pdec_excluded=false`、`direct_unconditional_contradiction_found=false`、`row_column_unconditional_closed=false`：第 `r` 层有效核心数满足 `M_r>=rho_0Y_0/prod_{i<=r}(A_iM_i)`，其中 `A_i<=8Lambda_i^2`、`M_i=max(b_i,c_i)<2Lambda_i`；阈值坍缩等价于 `prod(A_iM_i)>rho_0Y_0`，粗略充分生存条件为 `prod 16Lambda_i^3<=rho_0Y_0`。最早坍缩层之后若实际反例对象仍存在，它只能是有限深度、有限商型字母表中的稀疏终端历史；同一历史持久复现进入 `FixedTypeHistoryPDECExclusion`，非持久历史进入 `SparseTerminalDescentSAEAbsorptionLedger`。最新最窄点为 `SparseTerminalHistorySAEAbsorptionOrPDECExclusion`。 |
| strict 稀疏终端历史 SAE/PDEC 路由 | 新增/稀疏残留编码为有限历史词 | 新增 `experiments/prime_matrix_strict_sparse_terminal_history_router.py` 与 `docs/monograph/prime-matrix-strict-sparse-terminal-history-router.md/json`。审查确认 `history_word_encoding_closed=true`、`depth_cap_closed=true`、`alphabet_cap_closed=true`、`history_count_cap_closed=true`、`persistent_history_pdec_route_registered=true`、`nonpersistent_history_sae_reduction_closed=true`、`fixed_type_history_pdec_excluded=false`、`sparse_terminal_history_sae_budget_proved=false`、`sparse_terminal_history_absorbed=false`、`direct_unconditional_contradiction_found=false`、`row_column_unconditional_closed=false`：稀疏终端分支可编码为历史词 `W=((b_1,c_1),...,(b_r,c_r))` 加有限方向元数据；深度 `r<=floor(log_2|h_0|)`，每层字母表 `A_{Lambda_i}<=8Lambda_i^2`，历史总数 `<=sum_{r<=R}prod_{i<=r}A_{Lambda_i}`。同一历史词持久复现进入 `FixedTypeHistoryPDECExclusion`；若全部不持久，总残留质量压成 `SparseTerminalHistorySAEBudgetComparison` 与 `FormalUnitSparseHistoryMultiplicityCap`。最新最窄点为 `SparseTerminalHistorySAEBudgetComparison`，并列仍需固定历史 PDEC 排斥。 |
| strict 稀疏终端历史 SAE 预算比较 | 新增/非持久历史供需预算公式闭合 | 新增 `experiments/prime_matrix_strict_sparse_terminal_history_sae_budget_router.py` 与 `docs/monograph/prime-matrix-strict-sparse-terminal-history-sae-budget-router.md/json`。审查确认 `nonpersistent_sae_budget_formula_closed=true`、`history_count_inserted=true`、`forced_load_comparison_criterion_closed=true`、`sparse_terminal_pdec_or_budget_gap_closed=true`、`sparse_history_demand_exceeds_budget_proved=false`、`forced_load_lower_bound_proved=false`、`formal_unit_sparse_history_multiplicity_cap_proved=false`、`fixed_type_history_pdec_excluded=false`、`sparse_terminal_history_sae_budget_proved=false`、`direct_unconditional_contradiction_found=false`、`row_column_unconditional_closed=false`：若没有历史词 `W` 持久复现进入 PDEC，则 `U_sparse<=sum_W(T_PDEC(W)-1)Cap(W)`；历史数由 `sum_{r<=R}prod A_{Lambda_i}` 控制，若能证明早期零行强制负载 `L_forced` 大于该非持久供给预算，就得到供需矛盾。最新最窄点为 `SparseHistoryDemandExceedsNonpersistentSupplyBudget`，并行输入为 `SparseTerminalForcedLoadLowerBoundFromEarlyZeroRow`、`FormalUnitSparseHistoryMultiplicityCap`、`AdaptiveLambdaBalanceForIteratedCoreDensity` 与固定历史 PDEC 排斥。 |
| strict formal-unit 稀疏历史重数上界 | 新增/单历史容量压成缩频核心除数窗口 | 新增 `experiments/prime_matrix_strict_formal_unit_sparse_history_multiplicity_router.py` 与 `docs/monograph/prime-matrix-strict-formal-unit-sparse-history-multiplicity-router.md/json`。审查确认 `history_product_identity_closed=true`、`terminal_core_interval_closed=true`、`multiplicity_to_divisor_count_closed=true`、`hot_core_route_registered=true`、`cold_core_capacity_inserted=true`、`formal_unit_sparse_history_multiplicity_cap_proved=false`、`scaled_terminal_core_divisor_window_count_cap_proved=false`、`terminal_core_hot_divisor_window_excluded=false`、`fixed_type_history_pdec_excluded=false`、`direct_unconditional_contradiction_found=false`、`row_column_unconditional_closed=false`：对历史词 `W`，令 `D(W)=prod b_i c_i`，同一 formal unit 内该历史的终端核心必须满足 `k|h_0/D(W)` 且 `k in I_W`，所以 `Mult_U(W)<=N_{h_0/D(W)}(I_W)`。若该缩频除数窗口热，则回流 `TerminalCoreHotDivisorWindowPDECorSAE`；若不热，冷核心阈值 `C_core(W)` 可插入 SAE 预算。最新最窄点为 `ScaledTerminalCoreDivisorWindowCountCap`。 |
| strict 缩频终端核心除数窗口 | 新增/核心窗口冷热分裂接入统一矛盾场 | 新增 `experiments/prime_matrix_strict_scaled_terminal_core_divisor_window_router.py` 与 `docs/monograph/prime-matrix-strict-scaled-terminal-core-divisor-window-router.md/json`。审查确认 `scaled_core_frequency_closed=true`、`terminal_core_window_closed=true`、`cold_hot_split_closed=true`、`cold_core_budget_insertion_closed=true`、`hot_core_route_registered=true`、`scaled_terminal_core_divisor_window_count_cap_proved=false`、`terminal_core_hot_divisor_window_excluded=false`、`cold_core_threshold_budget_gap_proved=false`、`direct_unconditional_contradiction_found=false`、`row_column_unconditional_closed=false`：对历史词 `W`，定义缩频 `H_W=h_0/D(W)`，终端核心计数为 `N_{H_W}(I_W)=#{k:k|H_W,k in I_W}`。若 `N_{H_W}(I_W)<=C_core(W)`，则 `Cap(W)<=C_core(W)` 可进入 SAE 预算；若 `N_{H_W}(I_W)>C_core(W)`，则热核心窗口回流到 `TerminalCoreHotDivisorWindowPDECorSAE` 与 `TerminalCoreLCMHeightContradictionOrPDEC`。最新最窄点为 `ColdCoreThresholdBudgetGapComparison`，并列保留热核心回流排斥。 |

| strict 统一终端预算方程 | 新增/四段合成闭合，剩统一严格不等式 | 新增 `experiments/prime_matrix_strict_unified_terminal_budget_equation_router.py` 与 `docs/monograph/prime-matrix-strict-unified-terminal-budget-equation-router.md/json`。审查确认 `algebraic_composition_closed=true`、`prefix_rough_lower_reduction_imported=true`、`capacity_multiplier_normalization_imported=true`、`terminal_projection_balance_imported=true`、`cold_core_supply_bound_imported=true`、`unified_terminal_budget_strict_inequality_proved=false`、`b3_prefix_mass_inputs_proved=false`、`terminal_projection_inputs_proved=false`、`cold_supply_parameter_inputs_proved=false`、`direct_unconditional_contradiction_found=false`、`row_column_unconditional_closed=false`：统一方程为 `((P-1)W^-(z,D)-TV(lambda^-))/ceil(P/z)-E_named > sum_W(T_PDEC(W)-1)C_core(W)`；成立则 `L_forced>U_cold`，失败只能归入 B3/TV/有限证书、终端抗塌缩/命名回流、冷供给/Lambda 三组输入。最新最窄点为 `B3RemainderTotalVariationBudgetForLengthP`，并行保留 B3 主项、有限 prefix 证书、终端抗塌缩、命名回流排斥与冷供给参数平衡。 |

| strict 平方根核RH守门 | 新增/平方根核标记为RH-level，回到无条件psi表 | 新增 `experiments/prime_matrix_strict_sqrt_kernel_rh_guard_router.py` 与 `docs/monograph/prime-matrix-strict-sqrt-kernel-rh-guard-router.md/json`。审查确认 `sqrt_kernel_constant_arithmetic_fits_b28=true`、`sqrt_kernel_marked_rh_level=true`、`finite_verified_zero_window_controls_infinite_tail=false`、`verified_zero_sqrt_kernel_self_contained_closed=false`、`psi_epsilon_table_self_contained_closed=false`、`direct_unconditional_contradiction_found=false`、`row_column_unconditional_closed=false`：`1/(8pi)` 数值虽足以关闭 b=28 高尾，但对所有 `x>=e^28` 的平方根级 `psi` 误差属于 RH-level 输入；有限 verified-zero 窗口不能单独控制无限高尾。无条件主线不能用该核冒充作者侧闭合，下一最窄点回到 `SchoenfeldDusartPsiEpsilonTableInternalizationLedger`。 |
| strict Schoenfeld-Dusart psi表内化 | 新增/无条件表拆成四项输入与舍入审查 | 新增 `experiments/prime_matrix_strict_schoenfeld_dusart_psi_table_router.py` 与 `docs/monograph/prime-matrix-strict-schoenfeld-dusart-psi-table-router.md/json`。审查确认 `schoenfeld_dusart_psi_table_self_contained_closed=false`、`p51_arithmetic_splice_ready=true`、`table_rounding_margin_audit_closed=true`、`high_tail_eps_table_closed=false`、`middle_psi_upper_table_closed=false`、`psi_theta_gap_self_contained_closed=false`、`theta_less_than_identity_to_8e11_closed=false`、`direct_unconditional_contradiction_found=false`、`row_column_unconditional_closed=false`：无条件表路线需要 `eps_psi(28)<=0.00002224`、`8e11<=x<=e^28` 的 `psi<1.00002841x`、`psi-theta>0.9999sqrt(x)` 和 `theta(x)<x` 到 `8e11` 的有限表。中段拼接余量仅约 `4.46e-11`，表值生成器和舍入方向必须单独验收。下一最窄点为 `SchoenfeldDusartEpsilonTableGeneratorFormalizationLedger`。 |

| strict verified-zero/zero-free表输入 | 新增/来源边界闭合，缺大高度证书与尾项桥 | 新增 `experiments/prime_matrix_strict_verified_zero_zero_free_tail_input_router.py` 与 `docs/monograph/prime-matrix-strict-verified-zero-zero-free-tail-input-router.md/json`。审查确认 `zero_input_source_extraction_closed=true`、`verified_zero_and_zero_free_tail_input_closed=false`、`large_finite_rh_verification_table_input_closed=false`、`explicit_zero_free_region_table_tail_closed=false`、`finite_verified_zero_to_tail_transition_closed=false`、`low_height_t14_external_ready_but_insufficient=true`、`symbolic_zero_free_ready_but_numeric_table_tail_open=true`、`direct_unconditional_contradiction_found=false`、`row_column_unconditional_closed=false`：epsilon 表的零点输入不是仓库已有 `T<=14` 低高度核验，而是大高度有限 RH 验证、显式零点自由区尾项和二者的桥接算法共同支撑。已抽取 Rosser-Schoenfeld、van de Lune、Gourdon `10^13` 零点、Kadiri 零点自由区与 Dusart 表边界；仍缺可复现大高度零点证书、尾段零点自由常数和表生成桥接 hash。下一最窄点为 `LargeFiniteRHVerificationForPsiEpsilonTableLedger`。 |
| strict 大高度有限RH验证输入 | 新增/Gourdon外部可用，自足五包开放 | 新增 `experiments/prime_matrix_strict_large_finite_rh_verification_router.py` 与 `docs/monograph/prime-matrix-strict-large-finite-rh-verification-router.md/json`。审查确认 `large_finite_rh_external_source_identified=true`、`large_finite_rh_verification_self_contained_closed=false`、`external_gourdon_conditional_lane_available=true`、`large_zero_dataset_checksum_closed=false`、`large_zero_algorithm_closed=false`、`large_height_turing_completeness_closed=false`、`zero_index_to_height_mapping_closed=false`、`finite_rh_to_psi_table_interface_closed=false`、`direct_unconditional_contradiction_found=false`、`row_column_unconditional_closed=false`：外部路线可登记 Gourdon `10^13` 非平凡零点验证；作者侧自足路线必须补数据/hash、计算算法、Turing 完备性、零点编号到高度映射和表生成接口。`T<=14` 低高度包不能替代该输入。下一最窄点转为并列必需的 `ExplicitZeroFreeRegionForTableTailLedger`。 |

| strict finite-prefix/named-return 耦合正余量账本 | 新增/schema闭合，数值同参数证书仍开放 | 新增 `experiments/prime_matrix_strict_finite_prefix_named_return_coupled_margin_ledger_router.py` 与 `docs/monograph/prime-matrix-strict-finite-prefix-named-return-coupled-margin-ledger-router.md/json`。审查确认 `b3_external_lane_imported=true`、`coupled_margin_schema_closed=true`、`concrete_parameter_table_present=false`、`prefix_numeric_lower_bound_present=false`、`finite_boundary_prefix_open=true`、`finite_boundary_hash_present=false`、`named_return_numeric_bound_present=false`、`cold_supply_numeric_bound_present=true`、`sparse_history_demand_exceeds_budget_proved=false`、`persistent_named_return_excluded=false`、`nonpersistent_named_return_excluded=false`、`coupled_positive_margin_proved=false`、`direct_unconditional_contradiction_found=false`、`row_column_unconditional_closed=false`：`FinitePrefixNamedReturnCoupledPositiveMarginLedger` 的逻辑形态已闭合，最后必须生成 `ConcreteSameParameterMarginTableCertificate`，在同一 `parameter_id` 下列出 `D0_prefix_lower_bound`、`E0_named_return_deduction`、`U0_cold_supply_bound`、`margin_delta` 和 `finite_boundary_hash`。若 `margin_delta>0`，得到终端供需矛盾；若不为正，失败行必须登记为持久 PDEC/CleanKLS、非持久 SAE 超供给、热核心或固定历史 PDEC。 |
